# Kaggle run 39374324597 — paired replay evaluation and error audit
Date: 2026-10-20 (UTC)

## Scope and provenance

- GitHub Actions training run: [39374324597](https://github.com/hehohahifu83-cmyk/jitsuris-train/actions/runs/39374324597), success; Kaggle returned COMPLETE at 2026-10-20T05:48:45Z. Latest state capture run 39404684583 verified COMPLETE at 2026-10-20T09:59:37Z. `kaggle/kaggle.request=done`; no active GitHub Actions workflow at the current check.
- Training run artifacts are separate: evaluation reports artifact 12243449321 (1,423,122 bytes; SHA-256 `1fe031a632c5858ffbe4ffa848464417670c85c98aecf57257ea495da8f4c412`) and model artifact 12244070400 (868,010,919 bytes; SHA-256 `f2c0843a52e9056e4970ca2c8ff927a0dcb6c0092bb6c8dc52e9697ccee6428a`). The later status-capture run 39404684583 has no artifacts; it does not supersede the artifacts attached to the actual training run.
- The evaluation ZIP contains 210 members. This audit opened only `dev220`, `general`, `business`, `business2`, and `japan_procurement` members. No `blind_set*` or set 3, 5, or 7 members were opened or used.
- Four paired jobs: jobs 1/2 baseline, jobs 3/4 replay, seeds 1/2. Same e5-base, 2 epochs, UNIT context, batch 32 × accumulation 2, FT focal gamma 1.5; replay adds 39 rows (55,213→55,252). This is a fresh base-E5 fine-tune with a newly initialized sequence-classification head, not continuation from a prior Jitsuris checkpoint.

## Metric reconciliation

For all 4 jobs × 5 permitted sets × 5 conditions, all 100 metric cells in `FINETUNE_RESULTS.md` matched the evaluation artifact using the official `score_real` denominator (true `other` tracked separately). Error indices also matched the corresponding issue rows. No row-count mismatch remains for this artifact.

The table below compares FT-only official results. Counts combine the two seeds; critical errors are a subset of wrong predictions.

| Set | Accuracy baseline → replay (mean of seeds) | Δ pp | Wrong | Critical | Unknown |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| dev220 | 96.82% → 97.05% | +0.23 | 14 → 12 | 2 → 2 | 0 → 1 |
| general | 91.69% → 91.02% | -0.67 | 16 → 12 | 1 → 2 | 33 → 41 |
| business | 91.37% → 91.58% | +0.21 | 60 → 51 | 8 → 4 | 22 → 29 |
| business2 | 94.91% → 95.24% | +0.33 | 53 → 47 | 7 → 6 | 40 → 40 |
| japan_procurement | 98.91% → 98.37% | -0.54 | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 | 2 → 3 |
| Total across sets/seeds | — | — | 143 → 122 | 18 → 14 | 97 → 114 |

All five accuracy changes are below the predeclared 1.5 pp threshold. This is no clear accuracy difference; lower wrong counts coexist with 17 additional unknowns. In business2, `other_ng` rises from 13 to 25 while `other_ok` falls from 113 to 101. Do not describe the replay as a demonstrated overall capability gain.

Training loops: baseline seeds 570s/611s (mean 590.5s); replay seeds 583s/617s (mean 600.0s, +1.6%). Peak GPU memory: 5.5 GiB for all four jobs.

## Error / abstention observations

The artifact has row-level records for 1,105 replay issue instances across the two seeds, five allowed sets, and FT/blend50/blend60/blend70. The appendix below lists each issue instance. Instances repeat across seeds and conditions; these are not 1,105 unique source columns.

| Condition | Wrong | Critical | Unknown | true-other false accepts (`other_ng`) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| FT | 122 | 14 | 114 | 37 |
| blend50 | 74 | 11 | 209 | 14 |
| blend60 | 93 | 10 | 158 | 20 |
| blend70 | 112 | 11 | 120 | 32 |

Across the four conditions, the most common wrong-label transitions are category→quantity (92 instances), quantity→category (37), org→account (32), category→text (31), amount→quantity (25), and category→id (20). These are repeated across seeds and blend conditions, not unique-column counts.

FT-only row-level checks:
- 38/122 wrong predictions have raw top-1 probability ≥0.90. This confirms that high confidence alone does not prevent errors.
- 51 unknown outcomes have raw top-1 equal to the gold label, but a downstream guard abstained. The recorded reasons for FT unknowns are probability_below_threshold 37, other_class 33, date_value_evidence_low 30, numeric_values_not_parseable 11, and numeric_top2_too_close 3. This identifies the decision layer involved; it does not establish that the guard rule is wrong.
- Of 162 distinct dataset/index issue rows in FT, 111 recur as an issue in both seeds; 99 have the same outcome in both, 42 are wrong with the same raw predicted label, and 4 are critical in both. This is descriptive recurrence on the same reused evaluation rows, not independent generalization.

Source-record join was checked by allowed test-set index and gold label for the non-dev sources: all 1,050 repeated issue instances matched the source row's gold label. Source/header spelling differs because the exported model header is normalized. Source families represented in issue instances are: general—Gittables 185 / eGov 73; business—Data.gov UK 105 / eGov 16 / Canada 173 / Australia 44; business2—Data.gov UK 258 / Ireland 104 / eGov 8 / Canada 75; Japan procurement—JP 8 / JP2 1. Counts repeat across seeds/settings and cannot be interpreted as source error rates. The appendix/artifact does not retain raw cell values, exact UNIT-map matches, tokenizer token counts, truncation flags, or source-row IDs; cause attribution on these dimensions remains unconfirmed.

## Cause assessment: observations vs hypotheses

| Dimension | Observed fact | Hypothesis / confidence | Next check |
|---|---|---|---|
| Labels / definitions | Repeated category↔quantity and org↔account confusions occur in both seeds and blend variants. One replay example has a `税率` / `tax_class` conflict against generator/rules/guideline conventions (`tax_class` describes tax category; bare `税率` is rate). | Label-boundary ambiguity may contribute; medium confidence for the replay example, low for aggregate contribution. No labels changed. | Two teachers independently inspect the replay item and source evidence, then adjudicate. Scan all synthetic and municipality labels against guide/rules before any promotion. |
| Teaching material | Replay adds 39 rows to 55,213; only 9 are new ChatGPT single-auditor examples (~0.016% of baseline). Replay review metadata is one reviewer per example. | Too little, weakly audited data to reliably move the decision boundary; medium confidence. | Build source-diverse, reviewed contrast pairs only after two-person review; keep fixed eval rows out of training. |
| Duplicates / leakage | Exact duplicate signatures between the 110-row synthetic progress probe and replay corpus: 0. Their generators/vocabulary overlap by design. | Structural similarity may inflate the probe signal; possible, not quantified. The eval artifact lacks train/test source IDs, so it cannot rule out broader source overlap. | Audit source/table IDs and normalized model-input signatures across train/eval; use table/source-group splits. |
| Source distribution | Issue rows span multiple listed source families; counts are repeated by seed/condition. | Source-specific difficulty or shift cannot be separated from label mix using these repeated counts; low confidence. | Report unique-table, source-grouped errors on a locked new table set. |
| Header, values, UNIT, siblings | Category↔quantity and amount→quantity are common; unit-like sibling names appear in some joined context rows, but exact extracted UNIT context is absent from the artifact. | Missing/weak unit or sibling context could contribute; unproven. | Persist safe context-feature flags: unit match type, sibling count, header/value-shape summary, and truncation status per stable row ID. Do not persist cell values. |
| Preprocessing / tokenizer | Model input uses bounded header/value/sibling context; evaluation rows have no token-count/truncation fields. | Context truncation or representation loss could affect long/abbreviated columns; low confidence. | Add token length/truncation telemetry and compare one input-format factor at a time. |
| Model / confidence / guard | 38 FT wrongs are ≥0.90 top-1; 51 FT unknowns have raw top-1 equal to truth but are rejected downstream. Reasons are recorded above. | Calibration and guard interaction contribute to wrong/unknown tradeoffs; medium confidence that the decision layer causes those 51 abstentions, low confidence that a rule change improves overall safety. | Re-score using frozen row outputs; inspect all high-confidence wrongs and guard-rejected correct tops by reason. Tune only on train/validation, then evaluate locked tables. |
| Training settings / seed | Baseline and replay use the same FT_FOCAL_GAMMA=1.5 and other run settings; two seeds show incomplete recurrence. | Replay changes a subset of outcomes, but the small, seed-sensitive differences are consistent with weak/noisy signal; medium confidence. | Preserve every job checkpoint and use ≥5 matched seeds before adopting a data change. |
| Evaluation implementation | All 100 official metric cells and issue indices reconcile; the case output stores normalized headers and outcome/decision reason. | This run's aggregate mismatch risk is low. Earlier runs had cumulative error arrays; do not infer this run inherited that defect. | Keep one shared scorer and assert artifact counts on every run. |
| Speed / memory | Replay adds ~9.5s mean training time and no observed peak-memory increase (5.5 GiB in each job). | Small cost for no demonstrated accuracy gain. | Do not prioritize replay until a stronger, independently reviewed signal is available. |

## Prioritized actions

1. **P0 — preserve and identify artifacts per job.** Only job 1 had `FT_KEEP=1`; the returned model artifact is baseline job1/seed1, not replay. Preserve baseline/replay checkpoints with run, job, seed, dataset hash, and command metadata before the next experiment.
2. **P0 — case-level auditability.** Keep the shared scorer/count assertion. Add stable row/source IDs, raw/guarded/final decision, guard reason, confidence/margin, safe value-shape stats, UNIT-match flags, sibling count, token length/truncation, label/preprocess version. Never export test cell values.
3. **P1 — label quality.** Resolve the `税率` conflict only after two independent teacher reviews and evidence adjudication. No label edits before that gate.
4. **P1 — stronger learning test.** Current experiment is inconclusive for accuracy. Use matched multi-seed, source-grouped reviewed examples with one experimental factor and a locked new table test. Keep all current fixed suites development-only.
5. **Do not treat the progress probe as capability proof.** The 110 synthetic-row probe moved only +0.45 pp on average and shares synthetic generation patterns.
